# Visual-source provenance

Review date: November 13, 2026. [Library and limits](README.md). Exact page and media URLs are recorded below. Credential descriptions are publisher-reported, not a license audit.

## Publisher selection

<a id="publisher-nasm"></a>
### NASM

Professional fitness education and certification organization; official exercise pages and credited NASM channel. [Primary identity/context](https://www.nasm.org/workout-exercise-guidance).

Publisher authority does not validate every variation or resolve inconsistencies in an individual page.

<a id="publisher-puregym"></a>
### PureGym

Gym operator publishing its own trainer exercise demonstrations and technique guides. [Primary identity/context](https://www.puregym.com/exercises/).

Commercial training guidance. Some written pages have known errors retained in the technique library; multiple videos on a page can show different variants.

<a id="publisher-physitrack"></a>
### Physitrack

Clinical exercise-prescription publisher; describes expert-created, professionally narrated demonstrations. Provider metadata credits Physitrack. [Primary identity/context](https://www.physitrack.com/en-gb/exercise-library).

Library landing-page card labels can disagree with the linked video. Thirteen mismatched pairs were excluded. Clinical-library provenance is not an individual prescription.

<a id="publisher-rehabhero"></a>
### Rehab Hero

Clinician-led exercise library. Primary biography identifies David Song as a chiropractor and strength coach; selected embedded clips credit Rehab Hero. [Primary identity/context](https://www.rehabhero.ca/dr-david-song).

Biography is publisher-reported, not a license audit. Do not assume every unnamed model or contributor is the clinician. One mislabeled embedded clip was excluded; identical article titles can refer to different postures.

<a id="publisher-ms"></a>
### Muscle & Strength

Established exercise editorial publisher; selected original demonstrations are credited to its own channel and linked from exercise-specific instruction pages. [Primary identity/context](https://www.muscleandstrength.com/exercises/all.html).

Supplementary strength instruction. The selected pages do not establish the individual demonstrator credentials; some prose is inconsistent. No clinical authority inferred.

<a id="publisher-strongfirst"></a>
### StrongFirst

Strength education organization and original kettlebell coaching demonstrations. Windmill article identifies Brett Jones and his training qualifications. [Primary identity/context](https://www.strongfirst.com/kettlebell-windmill-101/).

Method-specific coaching choices; a title match is not evidence that every catalog variation or progression is shown.

<a id="publisher-trx"></a>
### TRX

Equipment manufacturer and official training channel. [Primary identity/context](https://www.trxtraining.com/).

Manufacturer expertise applies to its equipment; model-specific setup remains relevant. Compilation videos can contain multiple exercises.

<a id="publisher-hk"></a>
### Human Kinetics

Original sport and exercise textbook publisher with attributed instructional videos. [Primary identity/context](https://www.humankineticslibrary.com/custom-browse?docid=HumanKineticsStrengthandConditioningVideo).

Some library items require subscription. Selected public YouTube metadata was checked; full playback was not inspected.

<a id="publisher-onnit"></a>
### Onnit

Equipment and coaching publisher; named original demonstrations include Shane Heins and Aaron Guyett. [Primary identity/context](https://www.onnit.com/blogs/the-edge/9-battle-ropes-exercises-to-build-muscular-arms).

Commercial coaching reference. Kneeling, seated and in-and-out rope patterns must not be treated as standing or rotational variants.

<a id="publisher-gentilcore"></a>
### Tony Gentilcore / Testosterone Nation

Named strength coach original TRX anti-rotation article; embedded clip credited to Testosterone Nation rather than the article author channel. [Primary identity/context](https://tonygentilcore.com/2014/07/exercises-you-should-be-doing-trx-anti-rotation-press/).

Written setup was reviewed separately. Full video motion remains unreviewed; creator and host publisher are preserved separately.

<a id="publisher-steph"></a>
### Steph Gaudreau

Strength coach primary biography lists NASM-CPT and sports-nutrition credentials; selected clip credits her Fuel Your Strength channel. [Primary identity/context](https://www.stephgaudreau.com/steph-gaudreau/).

Publisher-reported credentials; no independent credential renewal check or full playback review.

<a id="publisher-e3"></a>
### E3 Rehab

Clinician education company founded by doctors of physical therapy; original exercise articles and credited E3 Rehab channel. [Primary identity/context](https://e3rehab.com/our-story/).

Rehabilitation context is not a prescription for app members. The pogo section start time comes from the article link, not observed playback.

<a id="publisher-atg"></a>
### ATG / Ben Patrick

Original coach and author of the Knee Ability Zero tibialis-raise method; primary coaching reference and credited The Kneesovertoesguy channel. [Primary identity/context](https://app.atgonlinecoaching.com/articles/article-chapter-1-of-the-knee-ability-zero-picture-book).

Coaching-method reference, not medical authority or evidence for broader injury-prevention claims.

<a id="publisher-bosu"></a>
### BOSU

Equipment manufacturer official named exercise demonstration. [Primary identity/context](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GtytyYHmrNM).

Pulse squat-jump variation is related only to a generic BOSU squat jump; surface orientation and transitions need comparison.

<a id="publisher-crossfit"></a>
### CrossFit

Original strength-and-conditioning education publisher and credited official channel. [Primary identity/context](https://www.crossfit.com/261003).

Sandbag demonstration only; no general endorsement of programming or individual suitability.

<a id="publisher-rogue"></a>
### Rogue Fitness

Equipment manufacturer original movement demonstration; provider description identifies athlete Matt Chan. [Primary identity/context](https://www.roguefitness.com/theindex/video/equipment-demo-sled-drag-variations-rogue-fitness-2?prerender=1).

Forward/backward drags are related to sled pulling; waist-harness and hand-over-hand rope versions differ. Direct page fetch was blocked, but indexed publisher page and official clip identity were checked.

<a id="publisher-qld"></a>
### Health and Wellbeing Queensland

Queensland public-health agency publishing its own exercise image and video. [Primary identity/context](https://hw.qld.gov.au/physical-activity/scapular-push-up-using-desk/).

Elevated desk support differs from a floor scapular push-up.

<a id="publisher-concept2"></a>
### Concept2

Rowing-machine manufacturer original technique demonstration and guidance. [Primary identity/context](https://www.concept2.com/training/rowing-technique).

Manufacturer technique reference; machine setup and individual adjustments still require comparison.

<a id="publisher-squatu"></a>
### Squat University

Aaron Horschig primary biography identifies a doctorate in physical therapy and strength/coaching background. [Primary identity/context](https://squatuniversity.com/about/).

Selected still identifies a movement starting posture only; not a complete observed hip-airplane sequence.

<a id="publisher-aaos"></a>
### AAOS OrthoInfo

American Academy of Orthopaedic Surgeons patient exercise illustrations. [Primary identity/context](https://orthoinfo.aaos.org/en/recovery/rotator-cuff-and-shoulder-conditioning-program/).

Cross-body shoulder figure only; medical rehabilitation context is not an individual prescription.

## Follow-up publisher context

[Per-target research and limits](research.md). Earlier publisher and media records above are retained as historical evidence.

<a id="publisher-opex"></a>
### OPEX Fitness

Professional fitness-coach education publisher; original exercise demonstrations are credited to the OPEX channel.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.opexfit.com/).

Coaching-education authority, not an individual clinical prescription. Speaker-specific licenses and full motion were not audited.

<a id="publisher-fbb"></a>
### Functional Bodybuilding

Original coaching publisher founded by Marcus Filly; primary biography describes affiliate ownership and individual coaching.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://functional-bodybuilding.com/pages/about).

Commercial coaching reference. Leaving medical school does not establish a medical qualification; no physician status is claimed.

<a id="publisher-matrix"></a>
### Matrix Fitness

Equipment manufacturer official setup, movement and operation demonstrations; provider metadata credits Matrix Fitness.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.matrixfitness.com/).

Machine geometry and controls are model-specific. Console operation is distinct from stepping-form review.

<a id="publisher-lifefitness"></a>
### Life Fitness / Hammer Strength

Equipment manufacturer official machine instruction demonstrations, credited to its Life Fitness / Hammer Strength channel.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.lifefitness.com/en-us/catalog/strength-training).

A specific machine model illustrates a configuration. The actual unit placard governs seat, pad and pivot adjustment.

<a id="publisher-specialolympics"></a>
### Special Olympics

Official sports-coaching organization publishing its own warm-up and cool-down exercise library and credited videos.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://resources.specialolympics.org/sports-essentials/sports-and-coaching/warm-up-and-cool-down-videos).

General coaching demonstrations, not individualized rehabilitation. Seated, standing and moving variants must be checked separately.

<a id="publisher-scottherman"></a>
### Scott Herman / Muscular Strength

Primary team biography identifies Scott Herman as a certified personal trainer; selected original clips credit ScottHermanFitness.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://muscularstrength.com/team).

Publisher-reported training background, not a current credential-renewal audit. A separate corroborating article is not claimed to embed his video.

<a id="publisher-fitnessfaqs"></a>
### FitnessFAQs

Original calisthenics coaching publisher. Primary biography identifies Daniel Vadnal and describes his physiotherapy background.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.fitnessfaqs.com/the-vault).

Background is publisher-reported; no current clinical license claim. A progression compilation is not a single-exercise instruction or full-motion approval.

<a id="publisher-dvids"></a>
### Marine Corps Force Fitness / DVIDS

Original military physical-fitness instruction distributed through DVIDS; selected pages identify Marine Corps Training and Education Command.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.dvidshub.net/video/642051/trx-pike), [source](https://www.dvidshub.net/video/692485/lateral-box-jump).

Physical-fitness education, not medical authority. Attribution and source copyright conditions remain applicable; no media redistribution is authorized.

<a id="publisher-rmp"></a>
### Rehab My Patient

Clinical exercise-prescription publisher; its primary site describes software designed by physiotherapists and a library of original exercise videos.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.rehabmypatient.com/).

Publisher-reported clinical context is not a license audit of every demonstrator or evidence of individual suitability.

<a id="publisher-origym"></a>
### OriGym

Professional personal-training education provider publishing its own attributed exercise demonstrations.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.origym.co.uk/about-us/), [source](https://www.origym.co.uk/courses/personal-trainer-courses/).

Provider-reported educational accreditation does not identify the credential of every model. Configuration and technique still need individual checking.

<a id="publisher-goodin"></a>
### Jacob Goodin

Original exercise demonstrations by Jacob Goodin; his university-hosted CV identifies a PhD in Sport Physiology and Performance.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.pointloma.edu/file/15881/download).

The CV establishes an educational background, not a medical license or current employment claim. Full video motion remains unreviewed.

<a id="publisher-horton"></a>
### Horton Barbell

Primary biography identifies Coach Horton as CSCS with a long collegiate strength-coaching and sports-science background.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://hortonbarbell.com/).

Publisher-reported qualifications, not a certification-renewal audit. External load and counterbalance variants must remain explicit.

<a id="publisher-pike"></a>
### Pike Fitness

Original training facility exercise demonstrations; primary staff page identifies Steve and Jeff Burmann as NSCA-CPT.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://pikefitness.com/).

Credentials are publisher-reported and not assigned to an unidentified model. Preview configuration checks are not full repetition or technique approval.

<a id="publisher-mbsc"></a>
### Mike Boyle Strength & Conditioning

Original strength-coaching facility exercise demonstrations; primary team biographies identify exercise-science education and CSCS-qualified coaches.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.bodybyboyle.com/team/coaches).

Team credentials do not identify every demonstrator. Direct page fetch was blocked; primary indexed biographies were reviewed.

<a id="publisher-champion"></a>
### Champion Physical Therapy and Performance

Original clinic exercise demonstrations; primary practice biography identifies physical therapists Mike Reinold and Lenny Macrina.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://championptandperformance.com/the-champion-difference/).

Clinical provenance is not a prescription. Direct page fetch was blocked; primary indexed biography and credited video metadata were reviewed.

<a id="publisher-brattain"></a>
### Nick Brattain

Original strength-coach demonstration; Tulane primary biography identifies Nick Brattain as CSCS with exercise-science education.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://sopa.tulane.edu/about-sopa/advisory-boards/nick-brattain).

Educational/coaching background is not an individual clinical prescription. Precise leg-drive timing remains unreviewed.

<a id="publisher-assault"></a>
### Assault Fitness

Equipment manufacturer original Assault University air-bike technique demonstrations, credited to the official Assault Fitness channel.

Primary identity/context: [source](https://www.assaultfitness.com/).

Technique clips illustrate the AirBike Classic. Saddle locks, controls and monitor settings differ on other air bikes.
